===== FILE scripts/module_release.py =====
"""Reviewed same-schema module rollout, with receipts and no force termination.

Host-only CLI, not a public API. Requires a reviewed manifest containing the
installed compose/ingress topology. Does not initialize DB, run migrations,
copy secrets, alter admission of unrelated pools or delete old artifacts.
On failure the target remains drained and the old peers keep serving; the scene
is never force-killed and never blindly retried.

An interrupted run (the process died before recording an outcome, leaving
`status=running`) is taken over only with an explicit `--recover-interrupted`,
from the same host, after proving the owning process is really gone.

A failed release is finished forward with `--recover-from <failed receipt.json>`
over the same reviewed manifest: it keeps the original release_id, re-verifies
every unit image/identity and the installed ingress on site, skips targets that
already finished, resumes the rest through the same rollout steps, and writes a
new receipt directory linking the failed receipt by hash. A completed release
reverses instead through rollback_plan.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from contextlib import contextmanager
import fcntl
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import subprocess
import time
import urllib.request
from datetime import datetime

SCHEMA = 135
CONTRACT = 'business-modules-v1'
MODULES = {'common', 'incremental', 'stock', 'compatibility'}
SINGLETON_ROLES = {'auxiliary', 'optimization', 'projection'}
LOCK_PATH = Path('/run/lock/label-module-release.lock')

# The descriptor of the held host release lock, and the file identity it was
# taken on; both None outside host_release_lock().
_LOCK_FD = None
_LOCK_ID = None


def lock_identity(handle=None):
    """Identity of the one fixed host lock, as the path and the descriptor see it.

    A mismatch means the lock file was unlinked and recreated while it was held:
    the flock we own is on an inode nobody else opens any more, so it excludes
    nothing and nothing may be concluded from holding it.
    """
    try:
        seen = os.stat(LOCK_PATH)
    except OSError:
        return None
    identity = {'path': str(LOCK_PATH), 'dev': seen.st_dev, 'ino': seen.st_ino}
    if handle is None:
        return identity
    held = os.fstat(handle)
    if (held.st_dev, held.st_ino) != (seen.st_dev, seen.st_ino):
        return None
    return identity


def holding_fixed_lock():
    """The identity of the fixed host lock this process actually holds, or None."""
    if _LOCK_FD is None:
        return None
    current = lock_identity(_LOCK_FD)
    return current if current == _LOCK_ID else None


@contextmanager
def host_release_lock():
    """Hold the single host release lock, at the one fixed LOCK_PATH, for one run.

    The descriptor is kept module-global so command() hands it to every child:
    if this process dies while a docker/nginx child is still running, that child
    keeps the same open file description and the lock stays held, so no second
    release starts on top of a half-applied step. Exiting only closes our own
    copy - never LOCK_UN, which would release the lock for those children too -
    and the lock file itself is never unlinked.
    """
    global _LOCK_FD, _LOCK_ID
    if _LOCK_FD is not None:
        raise RuntimeError('this process already holds the host release lock')
    path = LOCK_PATH
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    handle = os.open(path, os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT, 0o600)
    try:
        try:
            fcntl.flock(handle, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        except OSError as exc:
            raise RuntimeError('another module release holds the host lock; '
                               'a still-running child of a dead release also holds it') from exc
        identity = lock_identity(handle)
        if identity is None:
            raise RuntimeError('the host lock file was replaced while being taken; '
                               'it excludes nothing until it is stable')
    except Exception:
        os.close(handle)
        raise
    _LOCK_FD, _LOCK_ID = handle, identity
    try:
        yield handle
    finally:
        _LOCK_FD, _LOCK_ID = None, None
        os.close(handle)


def command(*args, timeout=60):
    # Children inherit the lock, so a child outliving its parent keeps it held.
    result = subprocess.run(args, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout,
                            pass_fds=() if _LOCK_FD is None else (_LOCK_FD,))
    return result.stdout.strip()


def host_identity():
    """Stable host identity, hashed: the machine-id never lands in a receipt in clear."""
    for path in ('/etc/machine-id', '/var/lib/dbus/machine-id'):
        try:
            value = Path(path).read_text().strip()
        except OSError:
            continue
        if value:
            return hashlib.sha256(('label-module-release/' + value).encode()).hexdigest()
    return None


def boot_id():
    try:
        return Path('/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id').read_text().strip() or None
    except OSError:
        return None


def process_start(pid):
    """Evidence about a pid, as (state, start_ticks).

    'running' carries the kernel start time, which separates a reused pid from
    the original. 'gone' means the kernel has no such process. 'unknown' means
    the evidence could not be read or parsed - a denied permission, an I/O
    error, a hidden pid namespace, a truncated stat line - and is never
    evidence that a process exited.
    """
    try:
        stat = Path('/proc', str(pid), 'stat').read_text()
    except (FileNotFoundError, ProcessLookupError):
        return ('gone', None)
    except OSError:
        return ('unknown', None)
    ticks = parse_start_ticks(stat)
    return ('running', ticks) if ticks else ('unknown', None)


def parse_start_ticks(stat):
    """Field 22 of /proc/<pid>/stat, read after the parenthesised comm.

    Returns None for anything that is not a plain decimal in that position, so a
    truncated or rewritten stat line is unreadable evidence rather than a value.
    """
    try:
        ticks = stat.rsplit(')', 1)[1].split()[19]
    except IndexError:
        return None
    return ticks if re.fullmatch(r'[0-9]+', ticks) else None


def owner_proof():
    """Who owns a run, provable after the fact.

    Only a run holding the host lock can produce a protected proof: a receipt
    written by a plain library call is readable but can never authorize taking
    over a still-running one.
    """
    lock = holding_fixed_lock()
    if lock is None:
        return {'protected': False,
                'reason': 'not holding the fixed host release lock, or it was replaced while held'}
    here = host_identity()
    if not here:
        return {'protected': False, 'reason': 'this host identity is unreadable'}
    pid = os.getpid()
    state, ticks = process_start(pid)
    if state != 'running':
        # We cannot even evidence our own start time, so nothing later may rely
        # on comparing it. Record that plainly instead of claiming a proof.
        return {'protected': False, 'reason': 'own process evidence is ' + state}
    return {'protected': True, 'host_sha256': here, 'boot_id': boot_id(),
            'pid': pid, 'start_ticks': ticks, 'lock': lock, 'at': time.time()}


def interrupted_owner(prior):
    """Prove the owner of a status=running receipt is gone, or refuse.

    Never kills and never assumes: a live owner keeps its release. The caller
    already holds the host lock, so no other release can start meanwhile - and
    a child outliving a dead owner still holds that lock, which is why this
    point is only reached once nothing of the old run is left running.
    """
    lock = holding_fixed_lock()
    if lock is None:
        raise RuntimeError('taking over an interrupted release requires holding the fixed host '
                           'release lock, unreplaced')
    owner = prior.get('owner') or {}
    pid = owner.get('pid')
    recorded = owner.get('lock')
    if not owner.get('protected') or not owner.get('host_sha256') \
            or not isinstance(owner.get('start_ticks'), str) or not owner['start_ticks'].isdigit() \
            or type(pid) is not int or pid <= 0 or not isinstance(recorded, dict):
        raise RuntimeError('the running receipt carries no lock-protected owner proof')
    if recorded.get('path') != lock['path']:
        raise RuntimeError('the running release was protected by a different lock file')
    here = host_identity()
    if not here:
        raise RuntimeError('cannot establish this host identity; take over on the release host')
    if owner['host_sha256'] != here:
        raise RuntimeError('the running release was started on another host')
    boot = boot_id()
    if not owner.get('boot_id') or not boot:
        raise RuntimeError('cannot compare boot identity; takeover needs a recorded and readable boot_id')
    if owner['boot_id'] == boot:
        # Same boot: the very same lock file must still be there. A replaced one
        # never excluded whoever now holds the new inode, so it proves nothing.
        if (recorded.get('dev'), recorded.get('ino')) != (lock['dev'], lock['ino']):
            raise RuntimeError('the host lock file was replaced since the release started; '
                               'it no longer proves the owner was excluded')
        # The recorded pid is comparable. A live process with the recorded start
        # time is the original owner and is left strictly alone.
        state, start = process_start(pid)
        if state == 'unknown':
            raise RuntimeError('the owner process evidence cannot be read; takeover needs proof '
                               'that it exited, never the absence of an answer')
        if state == 'running' and start == owner['start_ticks']:
            raise RuntimeError('the original release process is still running; it is never killed')
        reason = ('owner exited and its pid was reused by an unrelated process'
                  if state == 'running' else 'owner exited within the same boot')
    else:
        # A reboot recreates /run/lock on tmpfs, so a rebuilt lock file is expected.
        reason = 'host rebooted after the release started, so the owner cannot be running'
    return {'reason': reason, 'owner': owner,
            'observed': {'host_sha256': here, 'boot_id': boot, 'lock': lock, 'at': time.time()}}


def atomic(path: Path, content: str):
    temporary = path.with_name(path.name + '.next')
    temporary.write_text(content)
    os.replace(temporary, path)


def validate(plan):
    if plan.get('contract') != CONTRACT or plan.get('schema') != SCHEMA:
        raise ValueError('unsupported release/schema contract; migrations require an offline window')
    if plan.get('executor_revision') != 'runtime-v1':
        raise ValueError('unsupported executor contract')
    if not re.fullmatch(r'[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]{1,80}', plan.get('release_id', '')):
        raise ValueError('invalid release_id')
    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{40}', plan.get('source_sha', '')):
        raise ValueError('full source SHA required')
    if not re.fullmatch(r'sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}', plan.get('image', '')):
        raise ValueError('immutable local image ID required')
    units = plan['units']
    if not plan['targets'] or len(set(plan['targets'])) != len(plan['targets']):
        raise ValueError('nonempty unique targets required')
    for target in plan['targets']:
        unit = units[target]
        if unit['module'] not in MODULES or unit['kind'] not in {'api','executor','singleton'}:
            raise ValueError('invalid module unit')
        if unit['kind']=='singleton':
            if unit.get('role') not in SINGLETON_ROLES or unit['module']!='common':
                raise ValueError('invalid singleton role')
            if any(units[key]['container']==plan['control_container'] for key in plan['targets']):
                raise ValueError('singleton target must not be the control container')
            peers=[v for v in units.values() if v.get('kind')=='singleton' and v.get('role')==unit['role']]
            if len(peers)!=1:
                raise ValueError('singleton role must have exactly one unit')
        if not re.fullmatch(r'[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_.-]+', target):
            raise ValueError('invalid compose service')
        if unit['kind']=='executor' and unit['module'] not in {'incremental','stock','compatibility'}:
            raise ValueError('executor requires a business execution pool')
        if unit['kind'] == 'api':
            peers = [k for k,v in units.items() if v['kind']=='api' and v['module']==unit['module']]
            if len(peers) < 2:
                raise ValueError('API update requires a serving peer')
        if not re.fullmatch(r'sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}', unit['old_image']):
            raise ValueError('pinned old image required')
    if not set(plan['targets']).issubset(units):
        raise ValueError('unknown target')
    for path in [plan['env_file'], plan['nginx_upstreams'], *plan['compose_files']]:
        if not Path(path).is_absolute():
            raise ValueError('deployment paths must be absolute')
    if plan.get('shared_changes') is True and set(plan['targets']) != set(units):
        raise ValueError('shared change plan must include every dependent unit')


def render_upstreams(units, excluded=()):
    lines=[]
    for module in ('common','incremental','stock'):
        members=[u for key,u in units.items() if key not in excluded and u['kind']=='api' and u['module']==module]
        if not members:
            raise ValueError(f'no serving API peer for {module}')
        lines.append(f'upstream label_module_{module} {{')
        lines.append('    least_conn;')
        for unit in members:
            if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9.]+:[0-9]+', unit['address']):
                raise ValueError('numeric internal API address required')
            lines.append(f"    server {unit['address']} max_fails=1 fail_timeout=2s;")
        lines.extend(['    keepalive 16;', '}'])
    return '\n'.join(lines)+'\n'


def render_locations(prefixes):
    # OAuth callback contains one-use credentials. Do not retain its query in
    # nginx access logs (the application also redacts its access logger).
    lines=['location = /api/auth/dingtalk/callback {', '    access_log off;',
        '    add_header Referrer-Policy no-referrer always;',
        '    proxy_pass http://label_module_common;',
        '    include /etc/nginx/snippets/label-module-proxy.conf;', '}']
    for module in ('incremental','stock'):
        names=prefixes[module]
        if not names or any(not re.fullmatch('[a-z-]+',name) for name in names):
            raise ValueError('unsafe route prefix')
        lines.extend([f'location ~ "^/api/({"|".join(names)})(/|$)" {{',
            f'    proxy_pass http://label_module_{module};',
            '    include /etc/nginx/snippets/label-module-proxy.conf;', '}'])
    # Install these ordered regex locations before the static-file regex. A
    # plain /api/ prefix would let /api/assets/example.jpg fall into static 404.
    lines.extend(['location ~ "^/api/" {','    proxy_pass http://label_module_common;',
        '    include /etc/nginx/snippets/label-module-proxy.conf;', '}'])
    # Machine metrics is authenticated by the common API but lives outside
    # /api/. Without an exact route, the static SPA returns HTML with HTTP 200.
    lines.extend(['location = /metrics {', '    proxy_pass http://label_module_common;',
        '    include /etc/nginx/snippets/label-module-proxy.conf;', '}'])
    return '\n'.join(lines)+'\n'


def get(address, path):
    # Never replay business mutations; all readiness probes are GETs.
    with urllib.request.urlopen('http://'+address+path, timeout=5) as response:
        return json.load(response)


def wait_for(check, *, seconds, message):
    until=time.monotonic()+seconds
    last=''
    while time.monotonic()<until:
        try:
            value=check()
            if value:
                return value
        except Exception as exc:
            last=type(exc).__name__
        time.sleep(.5)
    raise RuntimeError(f'{message}; target retained, no forced kill; last probe={last}')


def nginx_workers():
    # Wait for actual old generations, not an arbitrary sleep or 503 retry.
    workers=set()
    for line in command('ps','-eo','pid=,args=').splitlines():
        parts=line.strip().split(None,1)
        if len(parts)==2 and parts[1].startswith('nginx: worker process'):
            try:
                stat=Path('/proc',parts[0],'stat').read_text()
                workers.add((int(parts[0]),stat.rsplit(')',1)[1].split()[19]))
            except FileNotFoundError:
                pass
    return workers


def inspect(container, *, allow_missing=False):
    try:
        data=json.loads(command('docker','inspect',container))[0]
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as exc:
        # Only explicit recovery may recreate a named target missing between
        # compose remove/create. Daemon/permission/transport errors are unknown,
        # not evidence of absence; all other units keep strict inspection.
        if allow_missing and exc.returncode==1 and (exc.stderr or '').strip() in {
            'Error: No such object: '+container,
            'error: no such object: '+container,
            'Error: No such container: '+container,
            'Error response from daemon: No such container: '+container,
        }:
            return {'id':None,'image':None,'started_at':None,'running':False,'missing':True}
        raise
    return {'id':data['Id'],'image':data['Image'],'started_at':data['State']['StartedAt'],
            'running':data['State']['Running']}


def runtime(plan, action='status', pool=None):
    args=['docker','exec',plan['control_container'],'python','-m','app.module_runtime',action]
    if pool:
        args+=['--pool',pool,'--release-id',plan['release_id']]
    return json.loads(command(*args))


def plan_digest(plan):
    """Canonical identity of a reviewed manifest.

    Recovery binds to this, not to a few fields: any edited target list, unit,
    pinned old image, compose/env/ingress path or route map is a different plan
    and needs its own review, even when release_id and image still match.
    """
    canonical=json.dumps(plan,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'),ensure_ascii=False)
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()


def fresh_worker(status, pool, source_sha, started_at):
    """A ready heartbeat from the process this rollout started, never a stale one."""
    since=datetime.fromisoformat(started_at.replace('Z','+00:00'))
    return any(worker['pool']==pool and worker['build_sha']==source_sha
        and worker['readiness']=='ready'
        and datetime.fromisoformat(worker['started_at'])>=since for worker in status['workers'])


def singleton_health(plan, target, state):
    unit=plan['units'][target]
    if not state['running'] or state['image']!=plan['image']:
        return False
    probe=Path(__file__).with_name('module_singleton_probe.py').read_text()
    facts=json.loads(command('docker','exec',unit['container'],'python','-c',probe,
                            unit['role'],state['started_at']))
    return (facts.get('healthy') is True and facts.get('role')==unit['role']
            and facts.get('build_sha')==plan['source_sha'])


def verify_candidate(plan):
    """Prove the reviewed image carries the reviewed source and contracts.

    Identical for a release and for its forward recovery: recovery may not skip
    a compatibility gate just because part of the fleet already runs the image.
    """
    image_meta=json.loads(command('docker','image','inspect',plan['image']))[0]
    env=dict(value.split('=',1) for value in image_meta['Config'].get('Env',[]) if '=' in value)
    # The build SHA is baked into the image by Dockerfile, never inferred from a tag.
    if env.get('LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA') != plan['source_sha']:
        raise RuntimeError('image/source SHA mismatch')
    candidate=json.loads(command('docker','run','--rm','--network','none',
        '-e','DATABASE_URL=sqlite://','--entrypoint','python',plan['image'],'-c',
        'import json; from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS; from app.module_api import PREFIXES; from app.module_runtime import SUPPORTED_EXECUTOR_REVISIONS; print(json.dumps({"schema":max(m.version for m in MIGRATIONS),"prefixes":PREFIXES,"revisions":SUPPORTED_EXECUTOR_REVISIONS}))'))
    if candidate != {'schema':SCHEMA,'prefixes':plan['prefixes'],'revisions':[plan['executor_revision']]}:
        raise RuntimeError('candidate schema/route/executor compatibility mismatch')
    status=runtime(plan)
    units=plan['units']
    target_pools={units[key]['module'] for key in plan['targets'] if units[key]['kind']=='executor'}
    if not status['configured'] or any(row['pool'] in target_pools for row in status['unsupported']):
        raise RuntimeError('unconfigured or unsupported queued execution contract')
    return status


class Rollout:
    """Per-unit rollout machinery shared by a release and its forward recovery.

    Recovery reuses these exact steps and only decides, from observed on-site
    facts, which steps a unit still needs; it never replaces a unit differently
    and never rolls a failed unit back.
    """

    def __init__(self, plan, root: Path, receipt, *, pins, excluded=()):
        self.plan=plan; self.root=root; self.receipt=receipt
        self.units=plan['units']
        self.receipt_path=root/'receipt.json'
        # Cumulative per-unit image pins are persisted beside the receipt. This
        # file is also the required final override for subsequent compose calls.
        self.pins_path=root/'image-pins.json'
        self.pins=pins
        # Which API units the proxy currently excludes. A recovery seeds this
        # from the installed ingress instead of assuming the original topology.
        self.excluded=set(excluded)
        self.ingress=Path(plan['nginx_upstreams'])
        self.compose=['docker','compose','--env-file',plan['env_file']]
        for file in plan['compose_files']:
            self.compose+=['-f',file]
        self.grace=plan.get('drain_timeout',900)

    def record(self, step):
        self.receipt['steps'].append({'at':time.time(),**step})
        atomic(self.receipt_path,json.dumps(self.receipt,indent=2))

    def apply_ingress(self):
        previous=self.ingress.read_text()
        atomic(self.ingress,render_upstreams(self.units,self.excluded))
        try:
            command('nginx','-t')
            command('nginx','-s','reload')
        except Exception:
            atomic(self.ingress,previous)
            command('nginx','-t')
            command('nginx','-s','reload')
            raise

    def exclude(self, target):
        self.excluded.add(target);self.apply_ingress()

    def include(self, target):
        self.excluded.discard(target);self.apply_ingress()

    def drain(self, target):
        unit=self.units[target];container=unit['container'];pool=unit['module']
        if unit['kind']=='api':
            for key,peer in self.units.items():
                if key!=target and peer['kind']=='api' and peer['module']==pool:
                    if get(peer['address'],'/api/health/serving')['status']!='serving':
                        raise RuntimeError('API peer not serving')
            old_workers=nginx_workers()
            if not old_workers:
                raise RuntimeError('cannot observe nginx workers for safe draining')
            self.exclude(target)
            wait_for(lambda: not (old_workers & nginx_workers()),
                seconds=self.grace,message='old nginx workers still serve the retired upstream')
            # A unit the failed attempt already stopped has nothing in flight; it
            # is drained by the ingress exclusion above, not by a forced kill.
            if inspect(container,allow_missing=self.receipt['mode']=='recovery')['running']:
                command('docker','exec',container,'python','-c',"from pathlib import Path; Path('/tmp/label-api-draining').touch()")
                wait_for(lambda:get(unit['address'],'/api/health/module')['active_requests']==0,
                         seconds=self.grace,message='API requests did not drain')
        elif unit['kind']=='singleton':
            # SIGTERM stops the role loop after its in-flight tick finishes.
            # The shared role flock stays held until that process exits; no
            # second role instance, global admission hold or proxy edit occurs.
            if inspect(container,allow_missing=self.receipt['mode']=='recovery')['running']:
                command('docker','stop','--time','-1',container,timeout=self.grace)
            if inspect(container,allow_missing=self.receipt['mode']=='recovery')['running']:
                raise RuntimeError('singleton still finishing its tick; refusing replacement')
        else:
            # Asserts this release still owns the pool; a foreign hold fails closed.
            runtime(self.plan,'drain',pool)
            wait_for(lambda:next(r for r in runtime(self.plan)['pools'] if r['pool']==pool)['leased']==0,
                     seconds=self.grace,message='model/checkpoint leases did not drain')
        self.record({'unit':target,'phase':'drained'})

    def pin(self, target=None):
        """Keep the required compose override current.

        Written once before any work and after every replacement, so a run that
        replaces nothing (a resume-only or already-finished recovery) still
        leaves the per-unit pins its receipt directory is supposed to carry.
        """
        if target:
            self.pins[target]['image']=self.plan['image']
        atomic(self.pins_path,json.dumps({'services':self.pins},indent=2))

    def replace(self, target):
        container=self.units[target]['container']
        if inspect(container,allow_missing=self.receipt['mode']=='recovery')['running']:
            # Infinite grace is deliberate. Admission is closed; never convert
            # a hung/slow model request into an unrecorded forced interruption.
            command('docker','stop','--time','-1',container,timeout=self.grace)
        if inspect(container,allow_missing=self.receipt['mode']=='recovery')['running']:
            raise RuntimeError('target is still running; refusing to recreate it')
        self.pin(target)
        # A same-image recovery must also discard the old writable layer, which
        # contains the API drain marker. Recreate only after graceful stop has
        # completed and inspect confirms it; never force-stop live business work.
        command(*self.compose,'-f',str(self.pins_path),'up','-d','--no-deps',
                '--no-build','--force-recreate',target,timeout=120)

    def verify(self, target):
        unit=self.units[target];container=unit['container'];pool=unit['module']
        if unit['kind']=='api':
            wait_for(lambda:get(unit['address'],'/api/health/serving')['status']=='serving',
                     seconds=180,message='replacement API not ready')
            facts=get(unit['address'],'/api/health/module')
            if facts['module']!=pool or facts['build_sha']!=self.plan['source_sha'] or facts['draining']:
                raise RuntimeError('replacement API identity mismatch')
        elif unit['kind']=='singleton':
            wait_for(lambda:singleton_health(self.plan,target,inspect(container)),
                     seconds=90,message='singleton has no fresh healthy role/lock/tick proof')
        else:
            # A DB probe proves the candidate can read the exact schema.
            command('docker','exec',container,'python','-c','from app.service_entry import validate_database; validate_database()')
            state=inspect(container)
            if state['image']!=self.plan['image'] or not state['running']:
                raise RuntimeError('replacement executor not running')
            wait_for(lambda:fresh_worker(runtime(self.plan),pool,self.plan['source_sha'],state['started_at']),
                     seconds=90,message='replacement executor has no fresh ready heartbeat')

    def restore(self, target):
        unit=self.units[target]
        if unit['kind']=='api':
            time.sleep(3)
            self.include(target)
        elif unit['kind']=='executor':
            # Only the owning release_id may reopen admission.
            runtime(self.plan,'resume',unit['module'])

    def update(self, target):
        self.drain(target);self.replace(target);self.verify(target);self.restore(target)


def drive(rollout: Rollout, work, before):
    """Run the decided per-unit work, then close the receipt or retain the scene."""
    receipt=rollout.receipt;units=rollout.units
    # The complete running receipt, owner proof included, is on disk before the
    # first change to the fleet: an interruption in the very first step still
    # leaves a record another run can be authorized to take over.
    rollout.record({'phase':'started','work':[{'unit':t,'action':a} for t,a in work]})
    try:
        rollout.pin()
        for target,action in work:
            if action=='completed':
                # Already carries the reviewed candidate and serves: never restart it.
                rollout.record({'unit':target,'phase':'already_completed',
                                'after':inspect(units[target]['container'])})
                continue
            getattr(rollout,action)(target)
            rollout.record({'unit':target,'phase':'updated','after':inspect(units[target]['container'])})
        receipt['after']={key:inspect(unit['container']) for key,unit in units.items()}
        for key in set(units)-{target for target,_ in work}:
            if receipt['after'][key]!=before[key]:
                raise RuntimeError(f'unrelated unit changed: {key}')
        receipt['status']='completed'
        rollout.record({'phase':'completed','image_pins':str(rollout.pins_path)})
    except Exception as exc:
        receipt['status']='needs_recovery'
        rollout.record({'phase':'stopped_safely','error':str(exc)})
        raise
    return receipt


def new_receipt_dir(root: Path):
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if (root/'receipt.json').exists():
        raise RuntimeError('receipt already exists; inspect it and create an explicit recovery/rollback plan')
    return root/'receipt.json'


def publish(plan, root: Path):
    validate(plan)
    new_receipt_dir(root)
    units=plan['units'];targets=plan['targets']
    before={key:inspect(unit['container']) for key,unit in units.items()}
    for target in targets:
        if before[target]['image'] != units[target]['old_image'] or not before[target]['running']:
            raise RuntimeError('installed unit differs from the reviewed manifest')
    verify_candidate(plan)
    ingress=Path(plan['nginx_upstreams'])
    old_ingress=ingress.read_text()
    if old_ingress != render_upstreams(units):
        raise RuntimeError('installed ingress differs from reviewed topology')
    (root/'upstreams.before.conf').write_text(old_ingress)
    receipt={'release_id':plan['release_id'],'source_sha':plan['source_sha'],'image':plan['image'],
        'schema':SCHEMA,'mode':'release','plan_sha256':plan_digest(plan),
        'owner':owner_proof(),'before':before,'steps':[],'status':'running'}
    rollout=Rollout(plan,root,receipt,pins={key:{'image':before[key]['image']} for key in units})
    return drive(rollout,[(target,'update') for target in targets],before)


def installed(plan, target, current, status):
    """Whether the reviewed candidate already runs on this unit with its identity."""
    unit=plan['units'][target]
    if current['image']!=plan['image'] or not current['running']:
        return False
    if unit['kind']=='api':
        try:
            if get(unit['address'],'/api/health/serving')['status']!='serving':
                return False
            facts=get(unit['address'],'/api/health/module')
        except Exception:
            return False
        return (facts['module']==unit['module'] and facts['build_sha']==plan['source_sha']
                and not facts['draining'])
    if unit['kind']=='singleton':
        try:
            return singleton_health(plan,target,current)
        except (OSError,subprocess.CalledProcessError,ValueError):
            return False
    return fresh_worker(status,unit['module'],plan['source_sha'],current['started_at'])


def restored(plan, target, excluded, pools):
    """Whether an installed unit already takes traffic/work again under no hold."""
    unit=plan['units'][target]
    if unit['kind']=='api':
        return target not in excluded
    if unit['kind']=='singleton':
        return True
    row=pools[unit['module']]
    return bool(row['accepting']) and not row['release_id']


def recover(plan, prior_path: Path, root: Path, *, interrupted=False):
    """Forward-complete a failed release from its receipt and the observed scene.

    Explicit by construction: it names the failed needs_recovery receipt, reuses
    the already reviewed manifest and its release_id, writes a new receipt
    directory and links the old receipt by hash. It never rolls a failed target
    back, never replays business work, never restarts a finished target and
    never assumes what the failed attempt completed.

    `interrupted=True` additionally accepts a receipt still marked running - a
    process that died without recording an outcome - but only after
    interrupted_owner() proves the owning process is gone on this same host.
    Every other gate stays exactly as strict.
    """
    validate(plan)
    prior_bytes=prior_path.read_bytes()
    prior=json.loads(prior_bytes)
    digest=hashlib.sha256(prior_bytes).hexdigest()
    # Whole-manifest binding: an unrecorded or edited plan fails closed here,
    # before anything about the previous run's owner is even considered.
    if (prior.get('schema')!=SCHEMA or prior.get('release_id')!=plan['release_id']
            or prior.get('plan_sha256')!=plan_digest(plan)):
        raise RuntimeError('recovery must reuse the reviewed plan of that failed release')
    takeover=None
    if prior.get('status')=='running' and interrupted:
        takeover=interrupted_owner(prior)
    elif prior.get('status')!='needs_recovery':
        raise RuntimeError('forward recovery requires a needs_recovery receipt; an interrupted '
                           'running receipt needs an explicit --recover-interrupted; completed '
                           'releases reverse through rollback_plan')
    new_receipt_dir(root)
    if root.resolve()==prior_path.resolve().parent:
        raise RuntimeError('forward recovery must write a new receipt directory')
    units=plan['units'];targets=plan['targets']
    status=verify_candidate(plan)
    # The pre-release scene travels down the whole recovery chain: every later
    # receipt keeps the images a rollback has to return to.
    origin=prior.get('origin_before') or prior['before']
    current={key:inspect(unit['container'],allow_missing=key in targets) for key,unit in units.items()}
    for key in set(units)-set(targets):
        if current[key]!=origin[key]:
            raise RuntimeError(f'unrelated unit changed since the failed release: {key}')
    for target in targets:
        if not current[target].get('missing') and current[target]['image'] not in {units[target]['old_image'],plan['image']}:
            raise RuntimeError('target carries an unknown image; recovery needs a separately reviewed plan')
    pools={row['pool']:row for row in status['pools']}
    for target in targets:
        unit=units[target]
        if unit['kind']=='executor' and pools[unit['module']]['release_id'] not in ('',plan['release_id']):
            raise RuntimeError('execution pool is held by another release')
    # The installed proxy decides the baseline; the original full upstream is not
    # assumed. Anything but the reviewed topology, or it minus one drained target,
    # is an unreviewed generation and fails closed.
    observed=Path(plan['nginx_upstreams']).read_text()
    options={frozenset():render_upstreams(units)}
    for target in targets:
        if units[target]['kind']=='api':
            options[frozenset([target])]=render_upstreams(units,[target])
    matched=[key for key,text in options.items() if text==observed]
    if len(matched)!=1:
        raise RuntimeError('installed ingress is neither the reviewed topology nor a single drained target')
    excluded=set(matched[0])
    (root/'upstreams.before.conf').write_text(observed)
    work=[];observations={}
    for target in targets:
        if installed(plan,target,current[target],status):
            action='completed' if restored(plan,target,excluded,pools) else 'restore'
        else:
            action='update'
        work.append((target,action))
        observations[target]={'image':current[target]['image'],'running':current[target]['running'],
                              'action':action}
    stopped=[step for step in prior.get('steps',[]) if step.get('phase')=='stopped_safely']
    receipt={'release_id':plan['release_id'],'source_sha':plan['source_sha'],'image':plan['image'],
        'schema':SCHEMA,'mode':'recovery','plan_sha256':plan_digest(plan),
        'owner':owner_proof(),
        'recovers':{'receipt':str(prior_path),'sha256':digest,'release_id':prior['release_id'],
                    'status':prior.get('status'),
                    'stopped_at':stopped[-1]['at'] if stopped else None,
                    'error':stopped[-1].get('error') if stopped else None,
                    'takeover':takeover},
        'origin_before':origin,'before':current,'observed':observations,
        'steps':[],'status':'running'}
    rollout=Rollout(plan,root,receipt,pins={key:{'image':plan['image'] if current[key].get('missing') else current[key]['image']} for key in units},
                    excluded=excluded)
    return drive(rollout,work,current)


def rollback_plan(plan, receipt, *, old_source_sha):
    """Create a concrete rollback of a completed compatible release.

    Incomplete releases retain their peers and admission flag. Their recovery
    is forward-only through recover(), from the recorded receipt and the
    observed scene, never guessed here.
    """
    if receipt['status']!='completed':
        raise ValueError('incomplete release needs state-specific forward recovery')
    # A recovery receipt's `before` is its own starting scene, in which targets may
    # already carry the candidate. Only the pre-release scene can be rolled back to.
    origin=receipt.get('origin_before') or receipt['before']
    old_images={origin[key]['image'] for key in plan['targets']}
    if len(old_images)!=1:
        raise ValueError('mixed old images require one explicit rollback plan per image')
    result=json.loads(json.dumps(plan))
    result.update(release_id=plan['release_id']+'-rollback',image=old_images.pop(),source_sha=old_source_sha)
    result['targets']=list(reversed(plan['targets']))
    for key,unit in result['units'].items():
        unit['old_image']=receipt['after'][key]['image']
    validate(result)
    return result


def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('manifest',type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--receipt-dir',type=Path,required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--apply',action='store_true')
    # Forward recovery is explicit: it names the failed receipt and reuses the
    # reviewed manifest, instead of rerunning the release over an unknown scene.
    parser.add_argument('--recover-from',type=Path,
        help='receipt.json of the failed release, status needs_recovery')
    # Taking over a run that never recorded an outcome is a separate, explicit
    # decision; it is never inferred from the receipt alone.
    parser.add_argument('--recover-interrupted',action='store_true',
        help='also accept a status=running receipt whose owning process is provably gone')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    if args.recover_interrupted and not args.recover_from:
        parser.error('--recover-interrupted only applies to --recover-from')
    plan=json.loads(args.manifest.read_text());validate(plan)
    mode='recovery' if args.recover_from else 'release'
    if not args.apply:
        print(json.dumps({'valid':True,'mode':mode,'targets':plan['targets'],
                          'image':plan['image'],'schema':SCHEMA,
                          'accepts_interrupted':bool(args.recover_interrupted)}));return
    # Shared host lock prevents two plans racing to rewrite the same proxy/pins,
    # and is what makes this run's own owner proof usable by a later takeover.
    with host_release_lock():
        result=recover(plan,args.recover_from,args.receipt_dir,
                       interrupted=args.recover_interrupted) if args.recover_from \
            else publish(plan,args.receipt_dir)
        print(json.dumps(result,indent=2))


if __name__=='__main__':main()
